Precompute structure only once per n:
```
def precompute_conv_indices(n):
    rows=[]; cols=[]
    for k in range(2*n-1):
        js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(n-1,k)
        rows.extend([k]*(je-js+1))
        cols.extend(range(js,je+1))
    return np.array(rows,dtype=np.int32), np.array(cols,dtype=np.int32)
```
For n=2048, total nonzeros = n(n+1) = 4,196,224? Actually sum row lengths = n(n+1). ~4.2M. Two int32 arrays ~33MB. Building once okay. But storing in dict? Top-level global cache.

```
c = -np.ones(n)
```

Objective fast: Need full convolution. Use `fftconvolve_exact` function. But final evaluate_sequence uses np.convolve. Our fast objective should match within. We can compute as:
```
def fast_objective(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01: return inf
    n=len(a)
    size=2*n-1
    N=1
    while N<size: N <<=1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:size]
    # small negatives from rounding
    m=conv.max()
    if m<0: m=0
    return 2.0*n*m/(s*s)
```

LP local search function:
```
def run_lp_search(a, deadline, t_initial=0.5, max_iters=None):
    n=len(a)
    struct = get_lp_structure(n)
    best=a; best_score=fast_objective(a)
    t=t_initial
    c_noimprove=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       conv = fast_conv(best); M=conv.max()
       A=make_conv_matrix(best, struct)
       res=linprog(...)
       if not res.x finite or len != n: break
       g=np.clip(res.x,0,None); normalize to sum best.sum()
       # candidate scores for t grid
       grid = [1.0, 0.75, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25, 0.15, 0.08]
       # maybe adapt t: include t and t*0.5, t*2
       best_cand=None; best_sc=best_score
       for tt in unique positive <=1:
          cand=(1-tt)*best + tt*g
          cand=np.maximum(cand,0); s=cand.sum(); if s>0 cand/=s
          sc=fast_objective(cand)
          if sc<best_sc: best_cand=cand; best_sc=sc
       if best_cand is not None:
          best=best_cand; best_score=best_sc
          # if best_cand used t near 1 or not, adapt; no need track? Find chosen tt.
          c_noimprove=0
          # print progress
       else:
          # reduce t or restart with perturbation
          t = max(0.02, t*0.6)
          c_noimprove +=1
          # add small smooth perturbation to escape
          if c_noimprove % 5 ==0:
             pert = smooth_perturb(best, rng, 0.1)
             # maybe try perturbation
             scp=fast_objective(pert)
             if scp < best_score: best=pert; best_score=scp
       if res.timed_out maybe continue? if many timeouts break
    return best,best_score
```

```
def _get_lp_structure(n):
    if n in _LP_CACHE: return _LP_CACHE[n]
    rows=[]; cols=[]; starts=[]
    cnt=0
    for k in range(2*n-1):
        starts.append(cnt)
        js = max(0, k-n+1); je = min(n-1,k)
        length = je-js+1
        # extend
        rows.extend([k]*length)
        cols.extend(range(js, je+1))
        cnt += length
    starts.append(cnt)
    arr = (np.asarray(rows,dtype=np.int32), np.asarray(cols,dtype=np.int32), np.asarray(starts,dtype=np.int64))
    _LP_CACHE[n]=arr
    return arr
```

Compute h_j = 2∑_k p_k a_{k-j}. Use p length 2n-1, a length n. As derived, h = 2 * conv(p, a_rev) slice. Let's implement with FFT:
```
def _convolve_pair(x,y): # full linear convolution x*y
   N = power of 2 >= len(x)+len(y)-1
   return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x,N)*np.fft.rfft(y,N),N)[:len(x)+len(y)-1]
```
For h: a_rev = a[::-1]; conv_pa = _convolve_pair(p, a_rev); h = 2*conv_pa[n-1:2*n-1]? Check length p 2n-1, a_rev n => total 3n-2. j = m-(n-1) where m=n-1+j, j0..n-1 => slice n-1 to 2n-1 inclusive length n. Good.

```
def smooth_dir(a, gamma):
   n=len(a)
   b=fast_conv(a)
   # ensure nonnegative
   scale = b.max()
   if scale<=0: return zeros
   beta = gamma/scale? Actually b max =M. Use beta = gamma*M. For gamma ~0.1.
   if beta <=0: idx argmax, p=onehot
   else: logits=b/beta; logits-=logits.max(); p=exp(logits); p/=p.sum()
   p=p/M? Wait gradient of β logsumexp(b/β): h = 2 * (p_b * a) where p_b_k = exp(b_k/β)/∑. This p sums1. Good. h units a, ~1/n.
   h = 2*_corr(p,a)
   # Normalize h by its max for step
   hmax = h.max()
   if hmax>0: h = h/hmax
   return h
```

Harmonic generator:
```
def _make_harmonic(n, seed):
  rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
  M = rng.integers(3,9)
  freqs = rng.choice? maybe 1..M
  amps = rng.uniform(0.1,0.6,M)
  phases = uniform(0,2π)
  x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
  w = 1.0
  for m in freqs: w += amps[i]*np.sin(2π*m*x + phases[i])
  # To ensure positive, add constant: w = 1 + 0.2? Actually w already base1 minus sum amps; min could negative. Clip.
  w = np.maximum(w, 1e-4)
  normalize
```
But if base1 and amps high, clipping introduces many zeros. Maybe use square: `w = 0.2 + np.square(1 + 0.5*Σ...)` to ensure nonnegative and can have shape. Simpler: `w = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(...)`? We'll just generate and clip.

LP solve direction with `linprog`:
```
def _lp_direction(a, deadline, lp_time_limit=45):
   n=len(a); a_pos=np.maximum(a,1e-9); a_pos = a_pos/a_pos.sum()
   conv = _fast_conv(a_pos)
   M = conv.max()
   amax = a_pos.max()
   a_scaled = a_pos / amax
   obj = -np.ones(n)
   b_ub = np.full(2*n-1, M / amax)
   # safety: if M/ amax <=0? no.
   diag = [np.full(n, a_scaled[d]) for d in range(n)]
   A = diags(diag, offsets=list(range(0,-n,-1)), shape=(2*n-1,n), format='csc')
   bounds = [(0,None)]*n
   t_limit = min(lp_time_limit, max(1, deadline-current-2))
   res = linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': t_limit})
   if res.x is None: return None
   g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
   # due to numerical, set tiny negatives 0, maybe also if g.sum() <=0 return None
   g = np.clip(g,0,None)
```

Let's implement `run_smooth(a, deadline, max_iters)`:
```
def run_smooth(a, deadline, t_initial=0.5, gamma=0.1):
    rng = _RNG
    best = a.copy(); best_score = fast_objective(best)
    base = a.copy()
    gamma = 0.2
    eta = 0.8
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       # anneal gamma: start larger (smooth), decrease to focus max
       gamma = 0.5 * np.exp(-it/500) + 0.02
       h = smooth_gradient(best, gamma)
       hmax = h.max()
       if not finite: break
       # candidates
       cands=[]
       for eta in [eta*2, eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25]:
           # multiplicative
           logw = np.log(best + 1e-300) - eta * (h/hmax)
           logw = logw - logw.max()
           cand = np.exp(logw); cand/=cand.sum()
           cands.append(cand)
           # linear projection
           v = best - eta*0.1*(h/hmax)? not scale? hmax ~? Use h normalized.
           cand2 = project_simplex(best - eta*0.2*(h/hmax)) # if h normalized to max1, subtract 0.2 changes relative
           cands.append(cand2)
       # evaluate
       best_local=None
       for cand in cands:
          sc=fast_objective(cand)
          if sc<best_score: best=cand; best_score=sc
       if improved: no_improve=0; # adapt eta increase? keep
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          eta = max(0.05, eta*0.7)
          if no_improve in (10, 50, 100, 200): perturb
       if it%100==0: print
    return best,best_score
```

Parameterization with tanh:
```
def _shape_from_params(params, n):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    f = np.zeros(n)
    K = len(params)-1
    for m in range(1,K+1):
        f += params[m-1] * np.sin(2*math.pi*m*x)
    rho = float(np.clip(params[-1], 0.0, 1.0))
    w = 1.0 + rho * np.tanh(f)
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```

Need project_simplex helper for linear updates. Implement efficiently:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    n=len(v)
    if v.ndim...; sort descending
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,n+1) > css - 1)[0]
    if len(rho)==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    r=rho[-1]
    theta=(css[r]-1)/(r+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
For v with large values, stable.

Smooth gradient cross-correlation:
```
def _cross_corr_p_a(p, a):
    n=len(a)
    a_rev = a[::-1]
    N=1
    size=2*n-1 + n -1 =3*n-2
    while N < size: N <<=1
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a_rev,N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(P*A,N)
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]  # length n
```
Wait slice: conv has length N; valid length 3n-2. Start n-1, length n -> indices n-1 ... 2n-2 inclusive. n=3, p len5, a_rev len3, conv len7. Start2 length3 indices2,3,4. Good.
But we derived h_j = 2*conv_pa[n-1+j]. Yes.

Smooth gradient:
```
def _smooth_gradient(a, gamma):
    b = _fast_conv(a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0)
    M = b.max()
    if M <=0: return zeros
    beta = gamma * M
    if beta <= 1e-300:
       p=zeros; p[argmax]=1
    else:
       z = b / beta
       z = z - z.max()
       p = np.exp(z)
       s=p.sum()
       if s<=0: p=onehot
       else: p = p/s
    h = 2 *_cross_corr_p_a(p,a)
    # due to numerical small negatives
    h = np.maximum(h,0) ? h should nonnegative. Clip.
    hmax = h.max()
    if hmax>0: h = h / hmax
    return h
```
Gamma: If gamma small, beta small, p peaked. If gamma large, p averaged over b. b values range 0..M; beta=γM. For γ=0.05, only top 5%; γ=0.5 broad. We can anneal γ from 0.5 (broad) to 0.02 (sharp). Good.

Potential cost: `_cross_corr_p_a` uses FFT length 3n; for n=4096, N=16384, okay. For many smooth phases maybe. But we can avoid if LP is primary.

Now, code for parameter shapes:
```
def _shape_odd(params, n, K):
    if n <= 1: return np.ones(1)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, min(K, len(params)) + 1):
        f = f + params[m-1] * np.sin(2.0*math.pi*m*x)
    if len(params) > K:
       rho = float(np.clip(params[K], 0.0, 0.95))
    else: rho=0.5
    w = 1.0 + rho * np.tanh(f)
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
    return _normalize(w)
```
For general:
```
def _shape_general(params,n,K):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    f=np.zeros(n)
    idx=0
    for m in range(1,K+1):
       f += params[idx]*np.sin(2π*m*x)
       if idx+1 < len(params)-1:
           f += params[idx+1]*np.cos(2π*m*x)
       idx += 2
    rho = params[-1] if len>0 else 0.5
    w=1+rho*tanh(f)
    ...
```
But if params length 2K+1. Good.

Could precompute x and sin/cos for speed? DE calls many; creating linspace each call overhead. We can cache basis for n and K. Top-level `_get_basis(n, K)` returns dict? But no closures. Function returns tuple of arrays. Use global `_BASIS_CACHE`.
```
def _get_basis(n, K):
    key=(n,K)
    if key in _BASIS_CACHE: return _BASIS_CACHE[key]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    sins=[]; coss=[]
    for m in range(1,K+1):
       theta=2π m x
       sins.append(np.sin(theta)); coss.append(np.cos(theta))
    res=(x,sins,coss)
    _BASIS_CACHE[key]=res
    return res
```
Then shape functions use basis. This speeds DE. For n=512,K=6, memory small. For n=4096, K maybe 6, memory 6*4096=24k. Fine.

```
def _run_lp(a0, deadline, max_no_improve=25):
    n=len(a0); a=a0.copy(); score=_fast_objective(a); best=a; best_score=score
    it=0; no=0; t_last=0.5
    rng_local = np.random.default_rng(999+it) # top helper uses global?
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
       if remaining < 2: break
       g = _lp_direction(a, max(5, min(60, remaining-5)))
       if g is None or g.sum() <=0:
           # try perturbation and continue? break
           break
       # cap outliers
       med = float(np.median(g))
       if med > 0:
          cap = 100.0*med
          g = np.clip(g,0,cap)
          s=g.sum()
          if s>0: g/=s
       # candidates
       best_cand=None; best_cand_score=score
       for tt in TS:
          cand = (1-tt)*a + tt*g
          cand = np.maximum(cand,0)
          ss=cand.sum()
          if ss > 0: cand /= ss
          sc=_fast_objective(cand)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_score:
              best_cand=cand; best_cand_score=sc
       if best_cand is not None:
          a=best_cand; score=best_cand_score
          if score < best_score:
             best=a.copy(); best_score=score
          no=0
          # maybe adjust? not needed
          it+=1
          if it%10==0: print
       else:
          no += 1
          # perturb a
          pert = _smooth_random_perturb(a, no)
          scp=_fast_objective(pert)
          if np.isfinite(scp):
             a=pert; score=scp
             if score < best_score:
                best=a.copy(); best_score=score
          if no >= max_no_improve:
             break
    return best, best_score
```

```
def _ensure_positive(a, rel=1e-7):
    a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return uniform
    a = a/s
    fl = rel*float(a.max())
    if fl >0: a = np.maximum(a, fl)
    a = a/a.sum()
    return a
```

Fast conv:
```
def _fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.array([])
    size = 2*n - 1
    N = 1
    # use bit length
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()  # careful size-1 when size1: 0.bit_length()=0 ->1
    spec = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(spec * spec, N)
    return out[:size]
```
Python int bit_length: `(size - 1).bit_length()`. For size=1, 0.bit_length()=0, 1<<0=1. Good. For size=3, 2.bit_length()=2, 4. Need N≥size, okay (4). For size=2,1.bit_length=1,N=2. Good.

In output maybe small negative; no issue.

`_fast_objective`:
```
def _fast_objective(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0: return float('inf')
    a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01: return inf
    b = _fast_conv(a)
    if b.size ==0: return inf
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return inf
    if m < 0: m=0.0
    score = 2.0 * float(a.size) * m / (s*s)
    if not np.isfinite(score): return inf
    return score
```

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64); a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0: return np.ones(a.size)/max(1,a.size)
    return a/s
```

```
def _get_basis(n, K):
    key=(n,K)
    if key in _BASIS_CACHE: return _BASIS_CACHE[key]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    sins=[]; coss=[]
    for m in range(1,K+1):
       th=2.0*math.pi*m*x
       sins.append(np.sin(th)); coss.append(np.cos(th))
    res=(x,sins,coss)
    _BASIS_CACHE[key]=res
    return res
```
For n=1, x [0], fine.

`_shape_odd(params,n,K)`:
```
if n <= 1: return np.ones(1)
_, sins, _ = _get_basis(n,K)
f = np.zeros(n)
K_eff = min(K, max(0,len(params)-1))
for i in range(K_eff):
   f = f + float(params[i]) * sins[i]
rho = 0.5
if len(params) > K_eff:
   rho = float(np.clip(params[K_eff], 0.0, 0.95))
w = 1.0 + rho * np.tanh(f)
w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
return _normalize(w)
```

`_shape_general(params,n,K)`:
```
_, sins, coss = _get_basis(n,K)
f=zeros; idx=0
for m in range(K):
   if idx < len(params)-1:
      f += float(params[idx]) * sins[m-1]
   if idx+1 < len(params)-1:
      f += float(params[idx+1]) * coss[m-1]
   idx += 2
rho = float(np.clip(params[-1],0,0.9)) if len(params)>0 else 0.5
w=1+rho*tanh(f); w=max; normalize
```

```
def _record(top_list, a, max_keep=40):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0: return float('inf')
    a = np.maximum(a,0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <=0 or not finite: return inf
    a = a/s
    score = _fast_objective(a)
    if not finite: return score
    top_list.append((score, int(a.size), a.copy()))
    if len(top_list) > max_keep:
       top_list.sort(key=_score_key)
       del top_list[max_keep:]
    return score
```
Need no lambda.

LP direction function uses `diags`. If `diags` not imported but HAVE_SCIPY true. Good.
```
def _build_conv_matrix_scaled(a):
    n=a.size
    data = [np.full(n, float(a[d]), dtype=np.float64) for d in range(n)]
    offsets = list(range(0, -n, -1))
    return diags(data, offsets=offsets, shape=(2*n-1,n), format='csc')
```

`_lp_direction(a, time_limit)`:
```
def _lp_direction(a, time_limit):
    n=a.size
    a = _ensure_positive(a, 1e-6)
    conv = _fast_conv(a)
    M = float(np.max(conv)) if conv.size else 0.0
    if M <=0: return None
    amax = float(np.max(a))
    if amax <=0: return None
    a_scaled = a / amax
    M_scaled = M / amax
    offsets=_get_offsets(n)
    diag=[]
    for d in range(n):
        val = float(a_scaled[d])
        diag.append(np.full(n, val, dtype=np.float64))
    A = diags(diag, offsets=offsets, shape=(2*n-1,n), format='csc')
    c = -np.ones(n)
    b_ub = np.full(2*n-1, M_scaled, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    # scale maybe if b_ub too small? HiGHS okay.
    options = {'time_limit': float(time_limit)}
    try:
       res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=options)
    except TypeError:
       res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds)
    except Exception:
       return None
    if res is None or getattr(res,'x',None) is None: return None
    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    if g.size != n or not np.isfinite(g).all(): return None
    g = np.maximum(g,0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12: return None
    # Clip outliers
    med = float(np.median(g))
    if med > 0:
        cap = 100.0 * med
        g = np.clip(g,0,cap)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if s > 0: g = g/s
    else:
        g = g/s
    return g
```
Potential: `linprog` may return `status` warnings; ignore. If `time_limit` 0, options weird; ensure >1.

```
def _run_smooth(a0, deadline):
    a = _normalize(a0.copy())
    best_score = _fast_objective(a); best = a.copy()
    it=0; no=0; eta=0.8; gamma=0.35
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if eta < 0.01: eta=0.5; no=0
       h = _smooth_gradient(a, gamma)
       if h is None or not np.isfinite(h).all(): break
       hmax = float(np.max(h)); if hmax <=0: break
       hn = h/hmax
       best_cand=None; best_cand_score=best_score
       for e in (eta*2, eta*1.5, eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25):
          # multiplicative
          loga = np.log(a + 1e-300) - e * hn
          loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
          c = np.exp(loga); cs = c.sum(); if cs>0: c/=cs
          sc = _fast_objective(c); update
          # relative linear
          v = a - e * (a * hn)
          c2 = _project_simplex(v); c2 = _normalize(c2)
          sc2; update
       gamma = 0.05 + 0.35 * math.exp(-0.003*it)  # anneal
       if best_cand is not None:
          a=best_cand; best_score_local? Actually update best if improved.
          no=0
          # perhaps adjust eta based on chosen? not track; okay
       else:
          no +=1
          eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.75)
          if no % 5 ==0:
              pert = _smooth_random_perturb(a)
              sc = _fast_objective(pert)
              if np.isfinite(sc):
                  a=pert
                  if sc < best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=sc
          if no > 500: break
       it +=1
       if it%200==0: print
    return best, best_score
```

Implement in `_cross_corr_p_a`:
```
def _cross_corr_p_a(p,a):
   n=a.size
   pm = float(p.max()) if p.size else 0
   if pm <=0: return zeros
   nz = np.nonzero(p > max(1e-12, 1e-6*pm))[0]
   if nz.size <= 32:
       h=np.zeros(n)
       for k in nz:
          wk=float(p[k])
          if wk<=0: continue
          lo=max(0, k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
          if lo <= hi:
              h[lo:hi+1] += (2.0*wk) * a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
       return h
   # FFT
```
Need ensure result length n. Direct formula: for fixed k, h_j += 2 p_k a_{k-j} for j valid. `lo=max(0,k-(n-1))`, `hi=min(n-1,k)`. Wait for h_j: j=0 index3, j=1 index2, j=2 index1, j=3 index0 => [a3,a2,a1,a0]. `a[k-hi : k-lo+1][::-1]`: k-hi=0, k-lo+1=4 => a[0:4][::-1]=[a3,a2,a1,a0]. Good.

```
def _select_best_n(top_list, n, count):
    out=[]

          out.append((score, a))

       if last is not None and abs(score-last) < 1e-9: continue
       res.append(a); last=score
       if len(res)>=count: break
    return res
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
except Exception:
    differential_evolution = None

try:
    from scipy.sparse import diags
except Exception:
    diags = None

HAVE_LP = (linprog is not None and diags is not None)
HAVE_DE = (differential_evolution is not None)

_LP_CACHE = {}
_BASIS_CACHE = {}
_MAIN_RNG = np.random.default_rng(20240527)

_DE_DEADLINE = 0.0
_DE_N_EVAL = 512
_DE_K_ODD = 6
_DE_K_GEN = 4

_LP_MIX = (0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20, 0.25,
           0.30, 0.35, 0.40, 0.50, 0.60,
           0.70, 0.80, 0.90, 1.00)


def _score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _pair_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        n = max(1, int(a.size))
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _ensure_positive(a, rel_floor=1e-7):
    a = _normalize(a)
    amax = float(np.max(a))
    if amax > 0.0:
        floor = float(rel_floor) * amax
        if floor > 0.0:
            a = np.maximum(a, floor)
            a = a / float(a.sum())
    return a


def _fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    spec = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(spec * spec, N)
    return out[:size]


def _fast_objective(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    b = _fast_conv(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    score = 2.0 * float(a.size) * m / (s * s)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return float("inf")
    return score


def _project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    good = np.nonzero(u * idx > (css - 1.0))[0]
    if good.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    r = int(good[-1])
    theta = (css[r] - 1.0) / float(r + 1)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)


def _get_basis(n, K):
    key = (int(n), int(K))
    if key in _BASIS_CACHE:
        return _BASIS_CACHE[key]
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    sins = []
    coss = []
    for m in range(1, int(K) + 1):
        th = 2.0 * math.pi * float(m) * x
        sins.append(np.sin(th))
        coss.append(np.cos(th))
    res = (x, sins, coss)
    _BASIS_CACHE[key] = res
    return res


def _shape_odd(params, n, K):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    _, sins, _ = _get_basis(n, K)
    f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    K_eff = min(int(K), max(0, int(len(params)) - 1))
    for i in range(K_eff):
        f = f + float(params[i]) * sins[i]
    rho = 0.5
    if len(params) > K_eff:
        rho = float(np.clip(params[K_eff], 0.0, 0.95))
    w = 1.0 + rho * np.tanh(f)
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
    return _normalize(w)


def _shape_general(params, n, K):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    _, sins, coss = _get_basis(n, K)
    f = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = 0
    last = max(0, int(len(params)) - 1)
    for m in range(int(K)):
        if idx < last:
            f = f + float(params[idx]) * sins[m]
        if idx + 1 < last:
            f = f + float(params[idx + 1]) * coss[m]
        idx += 2
    rho = 0.5
    if len(params) > 0:
        rho = float(np.clip(params[-1], 0.0, 0.95))
    w = 1.0 + rho * np.tanh(f)
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
    return _normalize(w)


def _make_sine(n, alpha, sign=1.0):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    w = 1.0 + float(sign) * float(alpha) * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * x)
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-6)
    return _normalize(w)


def _random_general_shape(n, seed, K=4):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    params = []
    for _ in range(int(K)):
        params.append(float(rng.uniform(-0.55, 0.55)))
        params.append(float(rng.uniform(-0.55, 0.55)))
    params.append(float(rng.uniform(0.30, 0.90)))
    return _shape_general(params, n, K)


def _smooth_random_perturb(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 2:
        return a
    rng = _MAIN_RNG
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    k1 = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
    k2 = int(rng.integers(2, 11))
    a1 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
    a2 = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.12))
    p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
    p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
    w = 1.0 + a1 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * float(k1) * x + p1)
    w = w + a2 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * float(k2) * x + p2)
    w = np.maximum(w, 0.5)
    return _normalize(a * w)


def _record(top_list, a, max_keep=50):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    score = _fast_objective(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return score
    top_list.append((score, int(a.size), a.copy()))
    if len(top_list) > max_keep:
        top_list.sort(key=_score_key)
        del top_list[max_keep:]
    return score


def _add_basic_candidates(top_list, deadline):
    sizes = (1024, 2048, 4096)
    alphas = (0.20, 0.40, 0.60, 0.80)
    for n in sizes:
        for alpha in alphas:
            for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    return
                a = _make_sine(n, alpha, sign)
                _record(top_list, a, 50)
        for seed in range(8):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                return
            a = _random_general_shape(n, 1000 * n + seed, K=4)
            _record(top_list, a, 50)
    prev = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    if prev is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(prev, dtype=np.float64)
            if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.isfinite(p).all():
                p = _normalize(p)
                _record(top_list, p, 50)
                for j in range(6):
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                        break
                    a = _smooth_random_perturb(p)
                    _record(top_list, a, 50)
        except Exception:
            pass


def _de_objective_odd(params):
    w = _shape_odd(params, _DE_N_EVAL, _DE_K_ODD)
    sc = _fast_objective(w)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e6
    return sc


def _de_objective_general(params):
    w = _shape_general(params, _DE_N_EVAL, _DE_K_GEN)
    sc = _fast_objective(w)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e6
    return sc


def _de_stop_callback(xk, *args, **kwargs):
    return time.monotonic() > _DE_DEADLINE


def _add_de_candidates(top_list, deadline):
    if not HAVE_DE:
        return
    global _DE_DEADLINE

    # Odd sine family: strong built-in reflection structure.
    _DE_DEADLINE = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 90.0)
    try:
        bounds = [(-1.0, 1.0)] * int(_DE_K_ODD)
        bounds.append((0.15, 0.90))
        res = differential_evolution(
            _de_objective_odd,
            bounds,
            maxiter=80,
            popsize=15,
            seed=11,
            polish=False,
            tol=1e-5,
            workers=1,
            callback=_de_stop_callback
        )
        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if x is not None:
            for n in (512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                _record(top_list, _shape_odd(x, n, _DE_K_ODD), 50)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # General low-order Fourier family.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
        _DE_DEADLINE = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 70.0)
        try:
            bounds = []
            for _ in range(int(_DE_K_GEN)):
                bounds.append((-0.65, 0.65))
                bounds.append((-0.65, 0.65))
            bounds.append((0.15, 0.90))
            res = differential_evolution(
                _de_objective_general,
                bounds,
                maxiter=60,
                popsize=12,
                seed=29,
                polish=False,
                tol=1e-5,
                workers=1,
                callback=_de_stop_callback
            )
            x = getattr(res, "x", None)
            if x is not None:
                for n in (512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                        break
                    _record(top_list, _shape_general(x, n, _DE_K_GEN), 50)
        except Exception:
            pass


def _get_offsets(n):
    key = ("offsets", int(n))
    if key not in _LP_CACHE:
        _LP_CACHE[key] = list(range(0, -int(n), -1))
    return _LP_CACHE[key]


def _lp_direction(a, time_limit):
    if not HAVE_LP:
        return None
    try:
        n = int(a.size)
        a = _ensure_positive(a, 1e-6)
        b = _fast_conv(a)
        M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            return None
        amax = float(np.max(a))
        if amax <= 0.0:
            return None
        a_scaled = a / amax
        m_scaled = M / amax

        offsets = _get_offsets(n)
        diag = []
        for d in range(n):
            val = float(a_scaled[d])
            diag.append(np.full(n, val, dtype=np.float64))
        A = diags(diag, offsets=offsets, shape=(2 * n - 1, n), format="csc")

        c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(m_scaled), dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

        opts = {"time_limit": max(1.0, float(time_limit))}
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds,
                          method="highs", options=opts)
        except TypeError:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds)

        if res is None:
            return None
        g = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if g is None:
            return None
        g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
        if g.size != n or not np.isfinite(g).all():
            return None
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        s = float(g.sum())
        if s <= 1e-14:
            return None

        med = float(np.median(g))
        if med > 0.0:
            cap = 100.0 * med
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, cap)
            s = float(g.sum())
        if s <= 1e-14:
            return None
        return g / s
    except Exception:
        return None


def _run_lp(a0, deadline):
    a = _ensure_positive(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64), 1e-6)
    current_score = _fast_objective(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = current_score
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 3.0:
            break

        g = _lp_direction(a, max(5.0, min(90.0, rem - 12.0)))
        if g is None:
            break

        best_cand = None
        best_cand_score = current_score

        for tt in _LP_MIX:
            cand = (1.0 - float(tt)) * a + float(tt) * g
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                continue
            cand = cand / s
            sc = _fast_objective(cand)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            if sc < best_cand_score * (1.0 - 1e-7) or sc < best_score:
                best_cand = cand
                best_cand_score = sc

        if best_cand is not None:
            a = best_cand
            current_score = best_cand_score
            stall = 0
            it += 1
            if current_score < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = current_score
            if it % 8 == 0:
                print("LP iter", it, "score", best_score)
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 6 == 0:
                pert = _smooth_random_perturb(a)
                sc = _fast_objective(pert)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    pert_improved = sc < current_score
                    a = pert
                    current_score = sc
                    if current_score < best_score:
                        best = a.copy()
                        best_score = current_score
                    if pert_improved:
                        stall = max(0, stall - 1)
            if stall > 32:
                break

    return best, best_score


def _cross_corr_p_a(p, a):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    pm = float(np.max(p)) if p.size else 0.0
    if pm <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    thresh = max(1e-12, 1e-6 * pm)
    nz = np.nonzero(p > thresh)[0]

    if nz.size <= 32:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in nz:
            k = int(k)
            wk = float(p[k])
            if wk <= 0.0:
                continue
            lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            if lo <= hi:
                h[lo:hi + 1] += (2.0 * wk) * a[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]
        return h

    size = 3 * n - 2
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    cr = np.fft.irfft(P * Ar, N)
    return cr[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _smooth_gradient(a, gamma):
    n = int(a.size)
    b = _fast_conv(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    M = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(gamma) * M
    if beta <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        k = int(np.argmax(b))
        p[k] = 1.0
    else:
        z = b / beta
        z = z - float(np.max(z))
        p = np.exp(z)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            p = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
            k = int(np.argmax(b))
            p[k] = 1.0
        else:
            p = p / s

    h = _cross_corr_p_a(p, a)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    hm = float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
    if hm > 0.0 and np.isfinite(hm):
        h = h / hm
    return h


def _run_smooth(a0, deadline):
    a = _normalize(np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64))
    current_score = _fast_objective(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = current_score
    gamma = 0.35
    eta = 0.9
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 1.0:
            break

        h = _smooth_gradient(a, gamma)
        if h is None or not np.isfinite(h).all():
            break
        hm = float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
        if hm <= 0.0:
            break
        hn = h / hm

        best_cand = None
        best_cand_score = current_score
        test_etas = (eta * 2.0, eta * 1.5, eta, eta * 0.5, eta * 0.25)

        for e in test_etas:
            if not np.isfinite(e) or e <= 0.0:
                continue

            loga = np.log(a + 1e-300) - e * hn
            loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
            c = np.exp(loga)
            cs = float(c.sum())
            if cs > 0.0 and np.isfinite(cs):
                c = c / cs
                sc = _fast_objective(c)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and (sc < best_cand_score * (1.0 - 1e-7) or sc < best_score):
                    best_cand = c
                    best_cand_score = sc

            v = a - e * (a * hn)
            c2 = _project_simplex(v)
            c2 = _normalize(c2)
            sc2 = _fast_objective(c2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and (sc2 < best_cand_score * (1.0 - 1e-7) or sc2 < best_score):
                best_cand = c2
                best_cand_score = sc2

        if best_cand is not None:
            a = best_cand
            current_score = best_cand_score
            stall = 0
            it += 1
            if current_score < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = current_score
            if it % 200 == 0:
                print("Smooth iter", it, "score", best_score)
        else:
            stall += 1
            eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.72)
            if stall % 80 == 0:
                gamma = min(0.55, gamma * 1.4)
            if stall % 25 == 0:
                pert = _smooth_random_perturb(a)
                sc = _fast_objective(pert)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    pert_improved = sc < current_score
                    a = pert
                    current_score = sc
                    if current_score < best_score:
                        best = a.copy()
                        best_score = current_score
                    if pert_improved:
                        stall = max(0, stall - 1)
            if stall > 1800:
                break

        gamma = 0.05 + 0.35 * math.exp(-0.0025 * it)

    return best, best_score


def _select_best_n(top_list, n, count):
    out = []
    for score, ln, a in top_list:
        if int(ln) == int(n):
            out.append((score, a))
    out.sort(key=_pair_key)
    res = []
    last_score = None
    for score, a in out:
        if last_score is not None and abs(float(score) - float(last_score)) < 1e-9:
            continue
        res.append(a)
        last_score = score
        if len(res) >= int(count):
            break
    return res


def _select_best_any(top_list, count):
    out = []
    top_list.sort(key=_score_key)
    last_len = None
    for score, ln, a in top_list:
        if last_len is not None and int(ln) == int(last_len) and len(out) >= 2:
            continue
        out.append(a)
        last_len = ln
        if len(out) >= int(count):
            break
    return out


def _finalize(top_list, deadline):
    top_list.sort(key=_score_key)
    best_seq = None
    best_official = float("inf")
    checked = 0
    for fast_score, n, a in top_list:
        if checked >= 6 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        seq = [max(0.0, float(x)) for x in a]
        if not seq:
            continue
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        except Exception:
            sc = float(fast_score)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if best_seq is None or sc < best_official:
                best_official = sc
                best_seq = seq
        checked += 1

    if best_seq is None:
        if top_list:
            best_seq = [max(0.0, float(x)) for x in top_list[0][2]]
        else:
            best_seq = [1.0]
    print("Final score:", best_official, "length:", len(best_seq))
    return best_seq


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 2400.0
    total = max(30.0, min(total, 2400.0))
    deadline_final = t0 + total - 22.0

    top_list = []

    if HAVE_LP:
        candidate_limit = t0 + 260.0
        candidate_deadline = min(candidate_limit, deadline_final - 720.0)
    else:
        candidate_limit = t0 + 140.0
        candidate_deadline = min(candidate_limit, deadline_final - 60.0)
    candidate_deadline = max(candidate_deadline, t0 + 12.0)

    _add_basic_candidates(top_list, candidate_deadline)
    _add_de_candidates(top_list, candidate_deadline)

    if not top_list:
        top_list = []
        for n in (1024, 2048, 4096):
            _record(top_list, _make_sine(n, 0.5, 1.0), 50)
            _record(top_list, _make_sine(n, 0.5, -1.0), 50)

    top_list.sort(key=_score_key)
    print("After init best fast score:", top_list[0][0] if top_list else float("inf"))

    if HAVE_LP:
        lp_window = max(0.0, float(deadline_final - t0))
        lp_limit = t0 + max(420.0, 0.62 * lp_window)
        lp_deadline = min(lp_limit, deadline_final - 140.0)
        lp_deadline = max(lp_deadline, t0 + 90.0)

        end1024 = min(lp_deadline, t0 + 220.0)
        for a in _select_best_n(top_list, 1024, 2):
            if time.monotonic() >= end1024:
                break
            try:
                out, sc = _run_lp(a, end1024)
                _record(top_list, out, 50)
                print("LP n=1024 best:", sc)
            except Exception:
                pass

        for a in _select_best_n(top_list, 2048, 2):
            if time.monotonic() >= lp_deadline:
                break
            try:
                out, sc = _run_lp(a, lp_deadline)
                _record(top_list, out, 50)
                print("LP n=2048 best:", sc)
            except Exception:
                pass

    smooth_deadline = deadline_final - 25.0
    if time.monotonic() < smooth_deadline:
        chosen = _select_best_any(top_list, 4)
        if chosen:
            avail = max(1.0, float(smooth_deadline - time.monotonic()))
            per = avail / float(len(chosen))
            for a in chosen:
                if time.monotonic() >= smooth_deadline:
                    break
                end = min(smooth_deadline, time.monotonic() + per)
                try:
                    out, sc = _run_smooth(a, end)
                    _record(top_list, out, 50)
                    print("Smooth best:", sc)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    return _finalize(top_list, time.monotonic() + 12.0)
```
